Görsel Prompt Kütüphanesi

Codex Enigmatica · 113 prompt · üretilen dosya (04_BUILD/plate_prompts.py) — elle düzenlemeyin, üreteç ezer.

0üretilmiş görsel
⚠ HİÇBİR GÖRSEL ÜRETİLMEDİ. 07_ASSETS/raw/ boştur. Görselleri kurucu üretir; bu dosya yalnızca ne üretileceğini söyler. Fiziksel POD provası (A9) da alınmadı.

2 · Ortak görsel üslup

⭑ LEVHA BİR RESİM DEĞİL, BULMACANIN VERİSİDİR ⭑
Her kartın “VERİ — DEĞİŞTİRİLEMEZ” bölümü bulmacanın kendi şeklinden üretilmiştir. Bir sayı değişirse o bulmaca çözülemez olur — ve bunu okur öğrenir, siz değil. Gravürcü nesneyi ve geometriyi çizer; işaretleri kitap dizer.

17th–18th century copperplate engraving. Fine parallel hatching and cross-hatching only — no grey wash, no soft shading, no colour. Pure black line on cream paper. Square-on, diagrammatic composition; the object fills the frame and is lit flatly so that no detail is lost to shadow. Thin ruled border. No text, no lettering, no numerals anywhere in the image.

3 · Ortak olumsuz kısıtlar

Gravür levhaları için

  • no text, letters, numerals or captions inside the image — every symbol the reader needs is typeset by the book, not drawn
  • no extra ornament: a mark the puzzle did not ask for is a mark the reader will try to read
  • no soft focus, blur, vignette or texture overlay — POD printing at 300 dpi loses them and the data with them
  • no perspective foreshortening on counted elements: if the reader must count them, they must all be the same size
  • no colour, no grey fills, no gradients

Kapak ve A+ için

no text of any kind — no title, no author name, no subtitle, no tagline, no caption, no page number, no signature; no letters, no words, no numerals, no invented script, no rune-like or glyph-like marks that a reader could try to decode; no logo, no publisher mark, no barcode, no ISBN block, no watermark, no QR code; no solved puzzle, no filled-in grid, no answer key, no marked solution path; no modern objects — no screens, no phones, no laptops, no plastic; no people, no faces, no hands; no fantasy clichés — no wizards, no dragons, no glowing runes, no magic particles, no arcane energy; no photorealism, no 3D render look, no CGI sheen, no HDR; no blur, no bokeh, no vignette, no film grain overlay, no dust scratches, no distressed texture filter; no colour banding, no neon, no oversaturation; no cluttered edges — the outer margin stays calm.

Kompozisyon

ONE SINGLE HORIZONTAL ROW of near-identical architectural or ritual objects standing on a common plinth, engraved as a specimen plate, seen straight on. Exactly one row — never a grid, never stacked rows, never a repeating wallpaper pattern. The number of objects is fixed by the data below and by nothing else. Their sameness is the point; a single one differs.

⭑ VERİ — DEĞİŞTİRİLEMEZ ⭑

  • exactly 5 of mark '◆' — countable, evenly spaced, all the same size, none overlapping
  • the engraved field is 5 bands tall

Nihai GPT Image promptu

ONE SINGLE HORIZONTAL ROW of near-identical architectural or ritual objects standing on a common plinth, engraved as a specimen plate, seen straight on. Exactly one row — never a grid, never stacked rows, never a repeating wallpaper pattern. The number of objects is fixed by the data below and by nothing else. Their sameness is the point; a single one differs. There are EXACTLY 5 marks of the form '◆' — count them; 5 and only 5. STYLE — 17th–18th century copperplate engraving. Fine parallel hatching and cross-hatching only — no grey wash, no soft shading, no colour. Pure black line on cream paper. Square-on, diagrammatic composition; the object fills the frame and is lit flatly so that no detail is lost to shadow. Thin ruled border. No text, no lettering, no numerals anywhere in the image. ⭑ IMMUTABLE DATA — these values are the puzzle itself and may NOT be changed, rounded, stylised or "improved": · exactly 5 of mark '◆' — countable, evenly spaced, all the same size, none overlapping · the engraved field is 5 bands tall The engraving must make every counted element separately countable at 300 dpi: equal size, equal spacing, no overlap, no foreshortening. ABSOLUTE CONSTRAINTS — no text, letters, numerals or captions inside the image — every symbol the reader needs is typeset by the book, not drawn; no extra ornament: a mark the puzzle did not ask for is a mark the reader will try to read; no soft focus, blur, vignette or texture overlay — POD printing at 300 dpi loses them and the data with them; no perspective foreshortening on counted elements: if the reader must count them, they must all be the same size; no colour, no grey fills, no gradients.

Kompozisyon

A FLAT ANNULAR DIAGRAM: a ring drawn as a plain circular band seen from DIRECTLY ABOVE — orthographic and perfectly circular, with no thickness, no depth, no rim, no interior wall, no cast shadow and no three-quarter view. The band is divided into equal wedge stations by plain radial rules, and every station is the same size because the ring is not tilted.

⭑ VERİ — DEĞİŞTİRİLEMEZ ⭑

  • exactly 7 of mark '┬' — countable, evenly spaced, all the same size, none overlapping
  • exactly ONE mark '▲' — a single, clearly separate incision
  • exactly ONE mark '◆' — a single, clearly separate incision
  • the engraved field is 7 bands tall

Nihai GPT Image promptu

Kompozisyon

Two walled enclosures side by side, each holding specimen pedestals; the pens are equal and plain.

  • exactly 3 of mark '○' — countable, evenly spaced, all the same size, none overlapping
  • the engraved field is 5 bands tall

Square-on, diagrammatic composition; the object fills the frame and is lit flatly so that no detail is lost to shadow. Thin ruled border. Kompozisyon

A FLAT ANNULAR DIAGRAM: a ring drawn as a plain circular band seen from DIRECTLY ABOVE — orthographic and perfectly circular, with no thickness, no depth, no rim, no interior wall, no cast shadow and no three-quarter view. The band is divided into equal wedge stations by plain radial rules, and every station is the same size because the ring is not tilted.

⭑ VERİ — DEĞİŞTİRİLEMEZ ⭑

  • the engraved field is 3 bands tall
  • exactly 7 stations around the band, each separated by a plain gap

6 · Kapak sanatı promptları · 2

YALNIZCA ÖN KAPAK. Sırt genişliği sayfa sayısından türer ve iç blok henüz dondurulmadı (K12 · Kapı V sayfa numaralarına bağlıdır). Sarmal kapak, seçilen ön kapak sanatından ve dizgi araçlarından sonradan kurulur. Bu kartlarda sırt ölçüsü YOKTUR ve olamaz.

SEÇENEK 01 — “BİLGİNİN MASASI” cover-option-01

Prompt kimliğicover-option-01
KonseptAn antiquarian puzzle table in a scholar's cabinet, arranged as a researcher would leave it at the end of a working day:…
Trim6 × 9 in · 2 : 3 (dikey)
Önerilen piksel1800 × 2700 px · 300 dpi · RGB
HAM dosya07_ASSETS/raw/codex-enigmatica-cover-option-01.png
Nihai hedef03_COVER/ · sarmal SONRADAN kurulur
Ticari dayanakBRIEF § 4.3 nesne değeri · § 4.1 meydan okuma
Ayrıntıyı göster

Kompozisyon notu

The table seen from slightly above, three-quarter angle. One strong focal object: an open book of engraved plates at the centre. Around it, in the order a scholar would leave them — a brass cipher wheel turned half a step, two copper plates laid one over the other, the edge of a folded map, a rule and a pair of dividers, and a row of BLANK wax seal impressions. Detail density FALLS from the centre toward the edges: the upper third and the lower fifth stay calm.

Sinyal

Zekâ · gizem · keşif · bilim · zarafet · meydan okuma · el işçiliği. Pahalı bir koleksiyoncu cildi gibi durur; bir çocuk etkinlik kitabı gibi DEĞİL.

Metin-güvenli alan

TOP 22% — a calm band for the title and subtitle: dark, flat, low-detail ground.
BOTTOM 15% — a calm band for the author name and series line.
Neither band may contain high-contrast detail that would fight typography set over it.

Nihai GPT Image promptu

An antiquarian puzzle table in a scholar's cabinet, arranged as a researcher would leave it at the end of a working day: engraved copper plates, cipher wheels, manuscripts, geometric puzzle objects, a folded map, astronomical markings, wax seals and fragments of a mechanical puzzle. COMPOSITION — The table seen from slightly above, three-quarter angle. One strong focal object: an open book of engraved plates at the centre. Around it, in the order a scholar would leave them — a brass cipher wheel turned half a step, two copper plates laid one over the other, the edge of a folded map, a rule and a pair of dividers, and a row of BLANK wax seal impressions. Detail density FALLS from the centre toward the edges: the upper third and the lower fifth stay calm. STYLE — Premium editorial illustration for an adult puzzle book. The look of a 17th–18th century scholar's cabinet photographed for a modern publishing catalogue: hand-inked copperplate linework over flat, restrained colour. Not fantasy art, not game-box art, not photorealistic, not 3D render, not cartoon. COLOUR — Restrained and warm: cream and aged paper, iron-gall ink black, deep indigo, oxblood red, tarnished brass, stone grey. No neon, no candy palette, no heavy gradients, no teal-and-orange grading. LIGHT — Soft, even editorial light from the upper left. Gentle contact shadows. No dramatic rim light, no god rays, no glow, no lens flare. TEXT-SAFE AREA — TOP 22% — a calm band for the title and subtitle: dark, flat, low-detail ground. BOTTOM 15% — a calm band for the author name and series line. Neither band may contain high-contrast detail that would fight typography set over it. Bu alan düz ve sakin kalır; tipografi sonradan dizilir. FORMAT — front cover art only, 6 × 9 in, 2 : 3 (dikey). Leave the text-safe zones described above visually calm. A+ GÖRSELİ BİR MANUSCRIPT SAYFASI DEĞİLDİR. Ürün sayfası herkese açıktır: oraya düşen bir cevap, kitabın içindekinden daha geniş yayılır. Hiçbir A+ görseli cevap, çözülmüş bir levha ya da okunabilir bir işaret gösteremez.
METİN AMAZON'DA, GÖRSELDE DEĞİL. Görsel yalnızca imgedir; başlık ve gövde metni Amazon'un kendi modül alanlarına girer. Böylece kopya değiştiğinde görsel yeniden üretilmez.
TİCARİ MESAJ UYDURULMADI. Her modülün “ticari dayanak” satırı BRIEF.md içindeki onaylı ifadeye işaret eder.

A final scene of resolution: a completed scholar's archive, an opened manuscript, engraved plates brought into alignment, a cipher wheel, and one SEALED, unopened envelope. COMPOSITION — Wide horizontal banner seen from slightly above. On the left, work that has been finished and put in order; on the right, standing alone, a sealed and UNOPENED envelope. The envelope is blank and bears no writing of any kind. The scene should feel like resolution while REVEALING nothing: the final answer is not printed in the book and is not printed here. STYLE — Premium editorial illustration for an adult puzzle book. The look of a 17th–18th century scholar's cabinet photographed for a modern publishing catalogue: hand-inked copperplate linework over flat, restrained colour. Not fantasy art, not game-box art, not photorealistic, not 3D render, not cartoon. 8 · ⭑ TAM SARMAL KAPAK — SIRADAKİ TESLİMAT ⭑ · 2

⚠ ELDEKİ KAPAK SANATI YALNIZCA ÖN KAPAKTIR. Bir ön kapağı gerip sarmal yapmak sırtı bulanık bir şerit, arka kapağı esnetilmiş bir kopya yapar. KDP bunu geri çevirir; çevirmese bile raf değeri biter. Sarmal ayrı bir sanat işidir.

⭑ NİHAİ PİKSEL ÖLÇÜSÜ BURADA YAZMIYOR — ÇÜNKÜ HENÜZ BİLİNMİYOR. Sarmal genişliği arka 6" + SIRT + ön 6" eder ve sırt genişliği sayfa sayısıyla kâğıt cinsinden türer. İç blok dondurulmadı (K12). Prompt "yeterince büyük sürekli yatay çözünürlük" ister; nihai kırpma ve ölçekleme CLI hattının işidir.

HAM konumu07_ASSETS/raw/ · 03_COVER/ DEĞİL — kurucu HAM verir, nihai kapağı ajan kurar
Ticari dayanakBRIEF § 4.3 nesne değeri · § 6.1 levhanın içindeki şifre
Ayrıntıyı göster

Kompozisyon notu

FULL BLEED CONTINUOUS WRAP read left to right as BACK, SPINE, FRONT — with NO visible panel boundary, NO triptych, NO seam and NO fold guide anywhere. Detail density RISES steadily from left to right so the eye is carried toward the front half. The surface material runs unbroken across the whole image.

Sinyal

Tek bir tarihsel yüzey. Kitap kapalıyken rafta bir cilt gibi durur; açıldığında tek bir gravür masası olduğu görülür.

Arka bölge

LEFT THIRD — quiet parchment-and-archive ground with only a few sparse engraved objects near the outer edge: one folded map corner, one small copper plate, one closed volume. The large central region of this third stays calm and open.

Sırt bölgesi

CENTRE VERTICAL STRIP — visually continuous with the surrounding leather and parchment. It is the SAME material passing through, never a drawn strip, never a blank white band, never a ruled panel. No object of importance sits on this axis.

Ön bölge

RIGHT THIRD — the strongest visual concentration: an open engraved puzzle folio as the focal object, with a brass cipher wheel, a geometric instrument, a sealed envelope, stacked copper plates and fragments of a mechanical puzzle arranged around it. This is where the composition resolves.

Metin-güvenli alanlar

BACK middle region — a calm low-detail area large enough for back-cover copy.
FRONT upper region — a calm band for the title.
FRONT lower region — a calm band for the author name.
SPINE centre — stable, even material for spine typography.
⭑ These zones must be calm GROUND, not drawn boxes: no panel, no frame, no plaque, no rectangle of flat colour.

CLI tipografi notu

Tipografi SONRADAN, CLI ile vektör olarak eklenir: ön başlık, yazar, sırt başlığı ve sırt yazarı. Sanat değiştirilmez; opak beyaz metin kutusu KULLANILMAZ. Bu yüzden güvenli alanlar gerçekten sakin olmalıdır.

One continuous antiquarian scholar's archive surface spanning the entire book wrap. Kompozisyon notu

FULL BLEED CONTINUOUS WRAP read left to right as BACK, SPINE, FRONT — one single map, NO panel divisions, NO triptych, NO visible fold guides. Engraved routes travel from the left edge across the whole surface and tighten toward a single convergence point in the front third.

Sinyal

“Bütün gizli sistem tek bir noktada birleşiyor.” Bilimsel ve editoryal; fantezi haritası ya da oyun haritası DEĞİL.

Arka bölge

LEFT THIRD — an antiquarian map and archive landscape: sparse engraved objects, subtle routes and a few isolated puzzle artifacts set well apart. Open, quiet ground dominates.

Sırt bölgesi

CENTRE VERTICAL STRIP — a natural vertical continuation of the map: one route running through it, one fine engraved border, a subtle central axis. NOT a blank strip, NOT an artificial spine panel, NOT a separate design.

Ön bölge

RIGHT THIRD — the map converges on a central intellectual object: a cipher apparatus with an engraved puzzle plate, a compass-like instrument, a sealed final document and a geometric mechanism. This reads as the point where the entire hidden system resolves.

Metin-güvenli alanlar

BACK middle region — a calm low-detail area for back-cover copy.
FRONT upper region — a calm band for the title.
FRONT lower region — a calm band for the author name.
SPINE centre — visually stable enough for later vector typography.
⭑ Routes and engraved lines must NOT cross these zones, and the zones must be calm GROUND — never a drawn panel.

CLI tipografi notu

Sırt genişliği ve kırım payları nihai sayfa sayısından türetilir; sanat o şablona CLI ile kırpılır. Önemli hiçbir öge kırım hattına oturmamalıdır.

9 · Kurucu teslim kontrol listesi

  1. Her prompt kartında prompt kopyala ile metni al — düğme yalnızca nihai promptu kopyalar, künyeyi değil.
  2. Görseli üret ve 07_ASSETS/raw/ altına kart üzerindeki adla kaydet.
  3. Gravürde hiçbir harf, rakam ya da uydurma işaret bulunmadığını gözle doğrula. Bir tane varsa görsel yeniden üretilir.
  4. “VERİ” bölümündeki her sayıyı görselde say. Tutmuyorsa görsel yeniden üretilir — veri pazarlığa kapalıdır.
  5. Kapakta ve A+ görselinde metin-güvenli alanın düz ve sakin kaldığını doğrula.
  6. Bittiğinde ./04_BUILD/qa_all.sh koştur.
⚠ SON SORUNUN CEVABI HİÇBİR GÖRSELDE GÖRÜNEMEZ. O cevap kitabın içinde de yazmaz; doğrulama sayfasına girilir.
